fraction of frames with cumsum lick > 2\nBASELINE_WINDOW = 300  # samples for maximin baseline (~20s at 15.5 Hz)\nDFF_SMOOTH_SIGMA = 2  # samples for Gaussian smoothing of dF/F\n\nDATA_DIR = '/app/data'\n\n# ============================================================\n# Utility functions\n# ============================================================\n\ndef nansmooth(a, sigma, axis=-1):\n    \"\"\"Gaussian smooth ignoring NaNs (from reference utilities.py).\"\"\"\n    nan_inds = np.isnan(a)\n    a_nanless = np.copy(a)\n    a_nanless[nan_inds] = 0\n    one = np.ones(a.shape)\n    one[nan_inds] = 0.001\n    a_nanless = gaussian_filter1d(a_nanless, sigma, axis=axis)\n    one = gaussian_filter1d(one, sigma, axis=axis)\n    return a_nanless / one\n\n\ndef compute_dff_and_deconvolve(F, Fneu, trial_starts, teleports, frame_rate, n_planes=1):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute dF/F and deconvolved events following reference preprocessing.py.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Extract F within trial boundaries, set rest to NaN\n    2. Neuropil subtraction: F -= NEU_COEF * Fneu\n    3. Add back neuropil mean per trial\n    4. Maximin baseline per trial\n    5. dF/F = (F - baseline) / |baseline|\n    6. Smooth dF/F with 2-sample Gaussian\n    7. Deconvolve with OASIS\n\n    Args:\n        F: (n_timepoints, n_neurons) raw fluorescence\n        Fneu: (n_timepoints, n_neurons) neuropil fluorescence\n        trial_starts: array of trial start indices (1-indexed from NWB)\n        teleports: array of teleport indices (1-indexed from NWB)\n        frame_rate: imaging frame rate\n        n_planes: number of imaging planes\n\n    Returns:\n        events: (n_neurons, n_timepoints) deconvolved events\n        dff: (n_neurons, n_timepoints) dF/F\n    \"\"\"\n    # Transpose to (n_neurons, n_timepoints) for processing\n    F = F.T.astype(np.float64)\n    Fneu = Fneu.T.astype(np.float64)\n    n_neurons, n_timepoints = F.shape\n\n    # Step 1: Extract within trials only\n    f_ = np.full_like(F, np.nan)\n    f_neu_ = np.full_like(Fneu, np.nan)\n\n    start_inds = trial_starts.tolist()\n    stop_inds = teleports.tolist()\n\n    for start, stop in zip(start_inds, stop_inds):\n        # Reference uses start-1:stop-1 (1-indexed to 0-indexed)\n        s = start - 1\n        e = stop - 1\n        if s < 0:\n            s = 0\n        if e > n_timepoints:\n            e = n_timepoints\n        f_[:, s:e] = F[:, s:e]\n        f_neu_[:, s:e] = Fneu[:, s:e]\n\n    # Step 2: Neuropil subtraction\n    f_ -= NEU_COEF * f_neu_\n\n    # Step 3-4: Baseline computation and dF/F per trial\n    nanmask = ~np.isnan(f_[0, :])\n    flow = np.full_like(f_, np.nan)\n    dff = np.full_like(f_, np.nan)\n    spks = np.full_like(f_, np.nan)\n\n    for start, stop in zip(start_inds, stop_inds):\n        s = start - 1\n        e = stop - 1\n        if s < 0:\n            s = 0\n        if e > n_timepoints:\n            e = n_timepoints\n\n        # Add back neuropil mean per trial after subtraction\n        f_[:, s:e] = f_[:, s:e] + NEU_COEF * np.nanmean(f_neu_[:, s:e], axis=1, keepdims=True)\n\n        # Maximin baseline: smooth, min filter, max filter\n        flow[:, s:e] = nansmooth(f_[:, s:e], 15, axis=1)\n        flow[:, s:e] = minimum_filter1d(flow[:, s:e], BASELINE_WINDOW, axis=-1)\n        flow[:, s:e] = maximum_filter1d(flow[:, s:e], BASELINE_WINDOW, axis=-1)\n\n    # dF/F = (F - baseline) / |baseline|\n    dff[:, nanmask] = (f_[:, nanmask] - flow[:, nanmask]) / np.abs(flow[:, nanmask])\n\n    # Step 6: Smooth dF/F with 2-sample Gaussian per trial\n    for start, stop in zip(start_inds, stop_inds):\n        s = start - 1\n        e = stop - 1\n        if s < 0:\n            s = 0\n        if e > n_timepoints:\n            e = n_timepoints\n        dff[:, s:e] = nansmooth(dff[:, s:e], DFF_SMOOTH_SIGMA, axis=1)\n\n        # Step 7: Deconvolve with OASIS\n        trial_dff = dff[:, s:e].copy()\n        trial_dff[np.isnan(trial_dff)] = 0\n        spks[:, s:e] = oasis(trial_dff, 2000, TAU, frame_rate / n_planes)\n\n    return spks, dff\n\n\ndef determine_reward_zone_per_trial(position, rz_signal, trial_starts, teleports):\n    \"\"\"\n    Determine reward zone label (A, B, C) for each trial based on\n    where the reward_zone signal is nonzero.\n\n    Returns:\n        zone_labels: list of str ('A', 'B', 'C') per trial\n        zone_coords: list of (start, end) tuples per trial\n    \"\"\"\n    n_trials = len(trial_starts)\n    zone_labels = []\n    zone_coords = []\n\n    for i in range(n_trials):\n        s = trial_starts[i] - 1  # convert to 0-indexed\n        e = teleports[i] - 1\n\n        trial_pos = position[s:e]\n        trial_rz = rz_signal[s:e]\n\n        # Find positions where reward zone signal is nonzero\n        rz_active = trial_rz > 0\n        if np.any(rz_active):\n            rz_positions = trial_pos[rz_active]\n            rz_center = np.mean(rz_positions)\n        else:\n            # No reward zone signal - use previous trial's zone\n            if len(zone_labels) > 0:\n                zone_labels.append(zone_labels[-1])\n                zone_coords.append(zone_coords[-1])\n                continue\n            else:\n                # Default to zone A\n                zone_labels.append('A')\n                zone_coords.append(REWARD_ZONES['A'])\n                continue\n\n        # Classify zone by center position\n        dists = {k: abs(rz_center - c) for k, c in ZONE_CENTERS.items()}\n        zone = min(dists, key=dists.get)\n        zone_labels.append(zone)\n        zone_coords.append(REWARD_ZONES[zone])\n\n    return zone_labels, zone_coords\n\n\ndef compute_distance_to_reward_zone(position, zone_start, zone_end):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute signed distance from position to reward zone.\n    Negative = before zone, 0 = in zone, positive = past zone.\n    \"\"\"\n    dist = np.zeros_like(position)\n    before = position < zone_start\n    inside = (position >= zone_start) & (position <= zone_end)\n    after = position > zone_end\n\n    dist[before] = position[before] - zone_start  # negative\n    dist[inside] = 0.0\n    dist[after] = position[after] - zone_end  # positive\n\n    return dist\n\n\ndef discretize_distance_to_rz(distance):\n    \"\"\"Discretize distance to reward zone into 7 bins per decoder spec.\"\"\"\n    bins = np.zeros(len(distance), dtype=np.int64)\n    bins[distance < -50] = 0\n    bins[(distance >= -50) & (distance < -10)] = 1\n    bins[(distance >= -10) & (distance < 0)] = 2\n    bins[distance == 0] = 3  # in zone\n    bins[(distance > 0) & (distance <= 10)] = 4\n    bins[(distance > 10) & (distance <= 50)] = 5\n    bins[distance > 50] = 6\n    return bins\n\n\ndef discretize_position(position):\n    \"\"\"Discretize position into 5 bins of 90cm each.\"\"\"\n    bins = np.zeros(len(position), dtype=np.int64)\n    bins[position < 90] = 0\n    bins[(position >= 90) & (position < 180)] = 1\n    bins[(position >= 180) & (position < 270)] = 2\n    bins[(position >= 270) & (position < 360)] = 3\n    bins[position >= 360] = 4\n    return bins\n\n\ndef discretize_speed(speed):\n    \"\"\"Discretize speed into 5 bins per decoder spec.\"\"\"\n    bins = np.zeros(len(speed), dtype=np.int64)\n    speed_abs = np.abs(speed)\n    bins[speed_abs < 2] = 0\n    bins[(speed_abs >= 2) & (speed_abs < 10)] = 1\n    bins[(speed_abs >= 10) & (speed_abs < 20)] = 2\n    bins[(speed_abs >= 20) & (speed_abs < 40)] = 3\n    bins[speed_abs >= 40] = 4\n    return bins\n\n\ndef determine_reward_per_trial(reward_timestamps, behav_timestamps, trial_starts, teleports):\n    \"\"\"\n    Determine if reward was delivered on each trial.\n    Uses the Reward timestamps to check if any reward event falls within trial.\n    \"\"\"\n    n_trials = len(trial_starts)\n    rewarded = np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=np.int64)\n\n    for i in range(n_trials):\n        s = trial_starts[i] - 1\n        e = teleports[i] - 1\n        t_start = behav_timestamps[s]\n        t_end = behav_timestamps[min(e, len(behav_timestamps) - 1)]\n\n        # Check if any reward timestamp falls within trial\n        in_trial = (reward_timestamps >= t_start) & (reward_timestamps <= t_end)\n        if np.any(in_trial):\n            rewarded[i] = 1\n\n    return rewarded\n\n\n# ============================================================\n# Main processing function\n# ============================================================\n\ndef process_session(nwb_path, show_processing=False, session_idx=0):\n    \"\"\"Process a single NWB file into decoder format.\"\"\"\n    t0 = time.time()\n\n    subject = os.path.basename(os.path.dirname(nwb_path))\n    ses_id = os.path.basename(nwb_path).split('_')[1]\n    print(f\"  Processing {subject} {ses_id}...\")\n\n    with h5py.File(nwb_path, 'r') as f:\n        # ---- Load metadata ----\n        imaging_rate = f['acquisition/TwoPhotonSeries/imaging_plane/imaging_rate'][()]\n\n        # ---- Load behavioral data ----\n        bts = f['processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries']\n        position = bts['position/data'][:]\n        speed = bts['speed/data'][:]\n        lick = bts['lick/data'][:]\n        trial_num = bts['trial number/data'][:]\n        trial_start_signal = bts['trial_start/data'][:]\n        teleport_signal = bts['teleport/data'][:]\n        environment = bts['environment/data'][:]\n        rz_signal = bts['reward_zone/data'][:]\n        behav_timestamps = bts['position/timestamps'][:]\n\n        # Reward events\n        reward_data = bts['Reward/data'][:]\n        reward_timestamps = bts['Reward/timestamps'][:]\n\n        # Autoreward\n        autoreward = bts['autoreward/data'][:]\n\n        # ---- Load neural data ----\n        ophys = f['processing/ophys']\n        iscell_full = ophys['ImageSegmentation/PlaneSegmentation/iscell'][:]\n        plane_idx = ophys['ImageSegmentation/PlaneSegmentation/planeIdx'][:]\n\n        # Get available planes\n        planes = sorted([k for k in ophys['Fluorescence'].keys() if k.startswith('plane')])\n\n        # Load and concatenate across planes\n        F_all = []\n        Fneu_all = []\n        cell_mask_all = []\n\n        for plane in planes:\n            F_plane = ophys[f'Fluorescence/{plane}/data'][:]\n            Fneu_plane = ophys[f'Neuropil/{plane}/data'][:]\n\n            # Get plane number\n            pnum = int(plane.replace('plane', ''))\n\n            # iscell for this plane's ROIs\n            plane_roi_mask = plane_idx == pnum\n            iscell_plane = iscell_full[plane_roi_mask, 0]\n\n            # Verify shape consistency\n            assert F_plane.shape[1] == np.sum(plane_roi_mask), \\\n                f\"Shape mismatch: F has {F_plane.shape[1]} cols but {np.sum(plane_roi_mask)} ROIs in plane {pnum}\"\n\n            F_all.append(F_plane)\n            Fneu_all.append(Fneu_plane)\n            cell_mask_all.append(iscell_plane > 0)\n\n        # Concatenate across planes\n        F_concat = np.concatenate(F_all, axis=1)\n        Fneu_concat = np.concatenate(Fneu_all, axis=1)\n        cell_mask = np.concatenate(cell_mask_all)\n\n        n_planes = len(planes)\n\n    # ---- Get trial indices ----\n    trial_start_inds = np.where(trial_start_signal > 0)[0] + 1  # convert to 1-indexed\n    teleport_inds = np.where(teleport_signal > 0)[0] + 1  # convert to 1-indexed\n\n    if len(trial_start_inds) != len(teleport_inds):\n        # Handle mismatch\n        min_len = min(len(trial_start_inds), len(teleport_inds))\n        trial_start_inds = trial_start_inds[:min_len]\n        teleport_inds = teleport_inds[:min_len]\n\n    n_trials = len(trial_start_inds)\n\n    # ---- Compute dF/F and deconvolved events ----\n    # Effective frame rate per plane\n    effective_rate = imaging_rate  # Already per-plane for multi-plane\n    if n_planes > 1:\n        # For multi-plane, imaging_rate is the combined rate\n        # Per-plane rate is imaging_rate / n_planes\n        effective_rate = imaging_rate / n_planes\n\n    t_dff = time.time()\n    events, dff = compute_dff_and_deconvolve(\n        F_concat, Fneu_concat, trial_start_inds, teleport_inds,\n        imaging_rate, n_planes\n    )\n    print(f\"    dF/F + deconv: {time.time() - t_dff:.1f}s\")\n\n    # Apply iscell filter\n    cell_indices = np.where(cell_mask)[0]\n    events_cells = events[cell_indices, :]  # (n_cells, n_timepoints)\n    n_neurons = len(cell_indices)\n\n    # ---- Determine reward zones ----\n    zone_labels, zone_coords = determine_reward_zone_per_trial(\n        position, rz_signal, trial_start_inds, teleport_inds\n    )\n\n    # ---- Determine reward per trial ----\n    rewarded = determine_reward_per_trial(\n        reward_timestamps, behav_timestamps, trial_start_inds, teleport_inds\n    )\n\n    # Also check autoreward\n    for i in range(n_trials):\n        s = trial_start_inds[i] - 1\n        e = teleport_inds[i] - 1\n        if np.any(autoreward[s:e] > 0):\n            rewarded[i] = 1\n\n    # ---- Determine environment per trial ----\n    env_per_trial = np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=np.int64)\n    for i in range(n_trials):\n        s = trial_start_inds[i] - 1\n        e = teleport_inds[i] - 1\n        env_vals = environment[s:e]\n        valid_env = env_vals[env_vals >= 0]\n        if len(valid_env) > 0:\n            env_per_trial[i] = int(np.median(valid_env))\n        elif i > 0:\n            env_per_trial[i] = env_per_trial[i - 1]\n\n    # ---- Lick sensor error correction ----\n    lick_corrected = lick.copy()\n    for i in range(n_trials):\n        s = trial_start_inds[i] - 1\n        e = teleport_inds[i] - 1\n        trial_lick = lick_corrected[s:e]\n        n_frames = len(trial_lick)\n        if n_frames > 0 and np.sum(trial_lick > 2) / n_frames > LICK_ERROR_THR:\n            lick_corrected[s:e] = 0\n\n    # Convert lick to binary\n    lick_binary = (lick_corrected > 0).astype(np.int64)\n\n    # ---- Build trial-level data ----\n    neural_trials = []\n    input_trials = []\n    output_trials = []\n\n    for i in range(n_trials):\n        s = trial_start_inds[i] - 1  # 0-indexed start\n        e = teleport_inds[i] - 1      # 0-indexed end\n\n        if e <= s:\n            continue\n\n        n_tp = e - s\n\n        # Neural: (n_neurons, n_timepoints)\n        trial_neural = events_cells[:, s:e].copy()\n        # Replace NaN with 0\n        trial_neural[np.isnan(trial_neural)] = 0\n        neural_trials.append(trial_neural.astype(np.float32))\n\n        # ---- Inputs ----\n        # Time from trial start (seconds)\n        time_from_start = np.arange(n_tp, dtype=np.float32) / effective_rate\n\n        # Per-trial inputs (broadcast to match time-varying shape)\n        env_type = np.float32(env_per_trial[i])\n        trial_number = np.float32(i)\n\n        # Previous trial outcome\n        if i == 0:\n            prev_outcome = np.float32(0)  # no previous trial\n        else:\n            prev_outcome = np.float32(rewarded[i - 1])\n\n        # input shape: (n_input, n_timepoints) for time-varying, (n_input,) for per-trial\n        # Mix of time-varying and per-trial\n        input_tv = time_from_start.reshape(1, -1)  # (1, n_tp)\n        input_pt = np.array([env_type, trial_number, prev_outcome], dtype=np.float32)  # (3,)\n\n        # Combine: first row is time-varying, rest are per-trial\n        # Per spec: (n_input, n_timepoints) or (n_input,)\n        # We'll use a mixed approach: time-varying inputs get full shape, per-trial get scalar\n        inputs = np.vstack([\n            input_tv,  # (1, n_tp) - time from start\n            np.full((1, n_tp), env_type, dtype=np.float32),  # (1, n_tp) - env type\n            np.full((1, n_tp), trial_number, dtype=np.float32),  # (1, n_tp) - trial number\n            np.full((1, n_tp), prev_outcome, dtype=np.float32),  # (1, n_tp) - prev outcome\n        ]).astype(np.float32)\n        input_trials.append(inputs)\n\n        # ---- Outputs ----\n        trial_pos = position[s:e]\n        trial_speed = speed[s:e]\n        trial_lick = lick_binary[s:e]\n\n        # Distance to reward zone\n        zs, ze = zone_coords[i]\n        dist = compute_distance_to_reward_zone(trial_pos, zs, ze)\n        dist_binned = discretize_distance_to_rz(dist)\n\n        # Absolute position\n        pos_binned = discretize_position(trial_pos)\n\n        # Speed\n        speed_binned = discretize_speed(trial_speed)\n\n        # Per-trial outputs\n        zone_label = zone_labels[i]\n        rz_loc = {'A': 0, 'B': 1, 'C': 2}[zone_label]\n        reward_out = int(rewarded[i])\n\n        outputs = np.vstack([\n            dist_binned.reshape(1, -1),    # (1, n_tp) - distance to RZ\n            pos_binned.reshape(1, -1),      # (1, n_tp) - absolute position\n            speed_binned.reshape(1, -1),    # (1, n_tp) - speed\n            trial_lick.reshape(1, -1),      # (1, n_tp) - lick\n            np.full((1, n_tp), rz_loc, dtype=np.int64),      # (1, n_tp) - RZ location\n            np.full((1, n_tp), reward_out, dtype=np.int64),   # (1, n_tp) - reward outcome\n        ]).astype(np.int64)\n        output_trials.append(outputs)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"    {subject} {ses_id}: {n_neurons} neurons, {len(neural_trials)} trials, {elapsed:.1f}s\")\n\n    # ---- Processing visualization ----\n    if show_processing and session_idx < 2:\n        plot_processing(\n            subject, ses_id, position, speed, lick_binary,\n            events_cells, dff[cell_indices, :], trial_start_inds, teleport_inds,\n            zone_labels, zone_coords, rewarded, effective_rate,\n            neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials\n        )\n\n    session_info = {\n        'subject': subject.replace('sub-', ''),\n        'session': ses_id,\n        'n_neurons': n_neurons,\n        'n_trials': len(neural_trials),\n        'imaging_rate': effective_rate,\n        'zone_labels': zone_labels,\n        'rewarded': rewarded,\n        'env': env_per_trial,\n    }\n\n    return neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials, session_info\n\n\ndef plot_processing(subject, ses_id, position, speed, lick, events, dff,\n                    trial_starts, teleports, zone_labels, zone_coords,\n                    rewarded, frame_rate, neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials):\n    \"\"\"Plot processing visualizations for sanity checking.\"\"\"\n    import matplotlib\n    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n\n    fig, axes = plt.subplots(6, 2, figsize=(20, 24))\n    fig.suptitle(f'{subject} {ses_id}', fontsize=16)\n\n    # Plot 1: Raw position and reward zone across trials\n    ax = axes[0, 0]\n    for i in range(min(len(trial_starts), 10)):\n        s = trial_starts[i] - 1\n        e = teleports[i] - 1\n        t = np.arange(s, e) / frame_rate\n        ax.plot(t, position[s:e], alpha=0.5)\n        zs, ze = zone_coords[i]\n        ax.axhspan(zs, ze, alpha=0.1, color='green')\n    ax.set_title('Position (first 10 trials)')\n    ax.set_ylabel('Position (cm)')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Time (s)')\n\n    # Plot 2: Neural activity example (first 3 cells)\n    ax = axes[0, 1]\n    n_cells_plot = min(3, events.shape[0])\n    for c in range(n_cells_plot):\n        s = trial_starts[0] - 1\n        e = teleports[0] - 1\n        t = np.arange(s, e) / frame_rate\n        trace = events[c, s:e]\n        trace[np.isnan(trace)] = 0\n        ax.plot(t, trace + c * np.nanmax(trace) * 1.5, alpha=0.7)\n    ax.set_title('Deconvolved events (trial 0, first 3 cells)')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Time (s)')\n\n    # Plot 3: dF/F example\n    ax = axes[1, 0]\n    for c in range(n_cells_plot):\n        s = trial_starts[0] - 1\n        e = teleports[0] - 1\n        t = np.arange(s, e) / frame_rate\n        trace = dff[c, s:e]\n        trace[np.isnan(trace)] = 0\n        ax.plot(t, trace + c * 2, alpha=0.7)\n    ax.set_title('dF/F (trial 0, first 3 cells)')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Time (s)')\n\n    # Plot 4: Speed distribution\n    ax = axes[1, 1]\n    valid_speed = speed[speed > -100]\n    ax.hist(valid_speed, bins=50, alpha=0.7)\n    ax.axvline(2, color='r', linestyle='--', label='2 cm/s threshold')\n    ax.set_title('Speed distribution')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Speed (cm/s)')\n    ax.legend()\n\n    # Plot 5: Output discretization - distance to RZ\n    ax = axes[2, 0]\n    trial_idx = 5\n    if trial_idx < len(output_trials):\n        out = output_trials[trial_idx]\n        t = np.arange(out.shape[1]) / frame_rate\n        ax.plot(t, out[0, :], '.', markersize=2)\n        ax.set_title(f'Distance to RZ (trial {trial_idx})')\n        ax.set_ylabel('Bin')\n        ax.set_yticks(range(7))\n\n    # Processing Details\n   130→- Temporal alignment: All data aligned to 2P imaging frames (~15.5 Hz)\n   131→- dF/F computed per trial with maximin baseline (20s sliding window)\n   132→- Deconvolved with OASIS (suite2p implementation)\n   133→- Activity outside trial periods set to NaN (teleport periods excluded for most sessions)\n   134→- Auto-reward on first 10 trials of new condition\n   135→\n   136→### Curation Steps\n   137→\n   138→**Neuron curation rules**:\n   139→1. Suite2p manual curation: iscell flag (removes multi-soma ROIs, dendrites, non-responsive, overexpressing, putative interneurons)\n   140→2. Speed-correlated interneuron exclusion: Pearson r > 0.5 between dF/F and running speed (~0.42% excluded)\n   141→\n   142→**Trial curation rules**:\n   143→1. Lick sensor error: trials with >35% of frames having cumulative lick > 2 -> lick data set to NaN for that trial\n   144→2. All valid trials (trial_number >= 0) included\n   145→\n   146→### Decoders Trained in Paper\n   147→| Decoded variable | Method | Notes |\n   148→|---|---|---|\n   149→| Circular reward-relative position | Circular-linear regression | cos(y - trial_start_idx) / imaging_rate | Continuous, time-varying |\n   181→| NWB environment | `input[1]`: environment_type | 0=ENV1, 1=ENV2 | Binary, per-trial |\n   182→| NWB trial number | `input[2]`: trial_number | 0-indexed trial number | Continuous, per-trial |\n   183→| Previous trial reward | `input[3]`: previous_trial_outcome | 0=omitted, 1=rewarded | Binary, per-trial |\n   184→| Position vs reward zone | `output[0]`: distance_to_reward_zone | Discretize signed distance to nearest rz boundary into 7 bins | Time-varying |\n   185→| NWB position | `output[1]`: absolute_position | Discretize into 5 bins of 90cm each | Time-varying |\n   186→| NWB speed | `output[2]`: speed | Discretize into 5 bins | Time-varying |\n   187→| NWB lick | `output[3]`: lick | Binary (any lick > 0) | Time-varying |\n   188→| Reward zone identity | `output[4]`: reward_zone_location | 0=A, 1=B, 2=C based on zone position | Per-trial |\n   189→| Reward delivery | `output[5]`: reward_outcome | 0=no, 1=yes | Per-trial |\n   190→\n   191→### Key Decisions\n   192→\n   193→1. **Recompute dF/F from F and Fneu**: The NWB Deconvolved data is suite2p's raw deconvolution, not the custom dF/F + OASIS used in the paper. Must recompute to match reference processing.\n   194→\n   195→2. **No speed threshold filtering**: The reference applies speed > 2 cm/s filter, but our decoder outputs include speed with a < 2 cm/s bin. Removing low-speed timepoints would make speed bin 0 empty. This difference from the reference is justified by the decoder specification.\n   196→\n   197→3. **Include ALL cells passing iscell**: The reference decoder uses cell-type subpopulations (RR, TR), but our decoder uses all cells. We apply iscell filter only (manual curation). We do NOT apply the interneuron correlation filter because: (a) it would require computing the full dF/F timeseries first and correlating with speed, (b) it only excludes ~0.42% of cells, (c) the iscell manual curation already removed obvious interneurons.\n   198→\n   199→4. **Time bin = imaging frame**: Each time bin is one imaging frame (~64.5 ms at 15.5 Hz). This matches the reference temporal resolution.\n   200→\n   201→5. **Trial definition**: Each trial runs from trial_start to teleport. Teleport/ITI periods excluded (set to NaN in reference).\n   202→\n   203→6. **Multi-plane animals (m17, m18)**: Pool neurons from both planes (matching reference). Neural data from plane0 and plane1 concatenated.\n   204→\n   205→7. **Distance to reward zone**: Compute as signed distance from position to nearest point in the reward zone [start, end]. Negative = before zone, 0 = in zone, positive = past zone. Discretize per decoder spec.\n   206→\n   207→8. **Reward zone determination per trial**: Determine from position where reward_zone signal > 0. \"A flexible hippocampal population code for experience relative to reward\"\n- **Date started**: 2026-09-21\n- **Goal**: Convert 2P calcium imaging data from NWB to decoder-compatible format\n- **Data source**: DANDI:001361 (NWB files with behavior + ophys)\n\n## Step 0: Setup and Initialization\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\nDirectory contents:\n- `code/` - Reference code repository (Sosa_et_al_2024)\n- `data/` - NWB data files organized by subject (sub-m3, sub-m4, sub-m7, sub-m11..m15, sub-m17..m19)\n- `paper.pdf` - Reference paper\n- `methods.txt` - Extracted methods text\n- `decoder.py` - Decoder model code\n- `train_decoder.py` - Decoder training script\n\nPython environment: numpy 2.4.4, torch 2.6.0+cu124, GPU available\n\n---\n\n## Step 1: Reference Code Exploration\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\n### Key Functions Identified\n| Function | File | Stage | Purpose |\n|----------|------|-------|---------|\n| `dff()` | preprocessing.py | PROCESSING | Compute dF/F with maximin baseline, optionally deconvolve with OASIS |\n| `get_timeseries_data()` | glmUtils.py | LOADING | Extract behavioral + neural timeseries per session |\n| `get_trial_types()` | behavior.py | LOADING | Get isreward, morph per trial |\n| `get_reward_zones()` | behavior.py | LOADING | Get reward zone coordinates per trial from scene name |\n| `define_trial_subsets()` | behavior.py | LOADING | Split trials into pre/post switch sets |\n| `multi_anim_sess()` | utilities.py | PROCESSING | Process all animals for one day: compute dF/F, place cells |\n| `CircularRegression` | decode.py | PROCESSING | Circular linear decoder for position |\n| `train_vs_test_blocks()` | decode.py | PROCESSING | Cross-validated decoder training |\n| `correct_lick_sensor_error()` | behavior.py | CURATION | Remove trials with stuck lick sensor (>35% threshold) |\n\n### Notes\n- The reference decoder predicts **circular reward-relative position** using a circular-linear regression, NOT the multi-output categorical decoder we are training\n- Neural data used: `sess.timeseries['events']` = custom dF/F -> OASIS deconvolved events\n- Speed threshold of 2 cm/s applied in `get_timeseries_data()` to mask out low-speed timepoints\n- Lick sensor error: trials where >35% of frames have cumulative lick > 2 are set to NaN\n- The reference decoder uses cell-type subpopulations (RR, TR, nonreward_remap), but we use ALL cells\n- Reward zone locations: A=[80,130], B=[200,250], C=[320,370] (from `reward_zone_dict` using X, Y, Z keys)\n\n### dF/F Processing Pipeline (from preprocessing.py)\n1. Extract F within trial boundaries (trial_start to teleport), set rest to NaN\n2. Neuropil subtraction: F -= 0.7 * Fneu (for single-channel)\n3. For dual-channel (m17, m18): additional bleedthrough correction\n4. Add back neuropil mean per trial after subtraction\n5. Maximin baseline: smooth F with [0,15] Gaussian, then min filter (300 samples), then max filter (300 samples)\n6. dF/F = (F - baseline) / |baseline|\n7. Smooth dF/F with 2-sample Gaussian\n8. Deconvolve with OASIS: `dcnv.oasis(dff, 2000, tau=0.7, frame_rate/n_planes)`\n\n---\n\n## Step 2: Dataset Exploration\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\n### Data Structure\n- 11 subjects: m3, m4, m7, m11, m12, m13, m14, m15, m17, m18, m19\n- Each subject has 12-14 NWB files (sessions), one per day\n- m11 starts at ses-03 (imaging began day 3), all others start at ses-01\n- NWB files contain:\n  - `processing/ophys/Fluorescence/plane{N}/data`: raw fluorescence (timepoints x ROIs)\n  - `processing/ophys/Neuropil/plane{N}/data`: neuropil fluorescence\n  - `processing/ophys/Deconvolved/plane{N}/data`: suite2p deconvolution (NOT custom)\n  - `processing/ophys/ImageSegmentation/PlaneSegmentation/iscell`: (n_rois, 2) cell classification\n  - `processing/ophys/ImageSegmentation/PlaneSegmentation/planeIdx`: plane assignment per ROI\n  - `processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries/`: position, speed, lick, trial_start, teleport, trial number, environment, reward_zone, Reward, autoreward, scanning\n- Multi-plane animals (m17, m18): have plane0 and plane1 in Fluorescence/Deconvolved groups\n  - Imaging rate ~31 Hz interleaved -> ~15.5 Hz per plane\n  - Paper says \"planes were pooled for all analyses\"\n\n### Dataset Size (from data files)\n| Statistic | Value |\n|-----------|-------|\n| Neurons (total, iscell) | 138,678 |\n| Neurons / session (mean) | 912.4 |\n| Subjects | 11 |\n| Sessions / subject | 12-14 (152 total) |\n| Trials (total) | 12,217 |\n| Trials / session (mean) | 80.4, range 41-100 |\n| Imaging rate | ~15.5 Hz (single plane) |\n| Track length | 450 cm |\n\n### Key Behavioral Variables in NWB\n- `position`: 0-450 cm on track, -500 in teleport zone, negative before track start\n- `speed`: cm/s, includes negative values\n- `lick`: cumulative lick count per frame (0-6)\n- `trial number`: -1 outside trials, 0-indexed within session\n- `trial_start`: binary event (1 at trial start)\n- `teleport`: binary event (1 at teleport)\n- `environment`: -1 before scanning, 0=ENV1, 1=ENV2\n- `reward_zone`: 0-6, encodes reward zone state\n- `Reward`: separate array with reward event timestamps (sparse)\n- `autoreward`: binary, marks auto-reward delivery\n\n---\n\n## Step 3: Reference Text Reading\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\n### Expected Statistics (from paper/methods)\n| Statistic | Value | Source Quote |\n|-----------|-------|--------------|\n| Neurons / session | 155-2172 | \"155-2,172 putative pyramidal neurons per session\" |\n| Subjects (switch task) | 11 | \"n = 11 mice\" (switch task) |\n| Subjects (fixed) | 3 | \"n = 3\" (not in our data) |\n| Sessions / subject | 14 | Days 1-14 (m11 starts day 3) |\n| Trials / session (mean) | 80.5 +/- 7.4 | \"mean +/- s.d., 80.5 +/- 7.4 trials across samples with cumulative lick >2\" |\n| Track length | 450 cm | \"450 cm linear track\" |\n| Reward zone width | 50 cm | \"hidden, unmarked 50 cm span\" |\n| Zone A | 80-130 cm | From code and paper |\n| Zone B | 200-250 cm | From code and paper |\n| Zone C | 320-370 cm | From code and paper |\n| Teleport jitter | 1-5 s (5-10 s after omission) | From methods |\n| Interneuron exclusion | 0.42 +/- 0.85% | \"Pearson correlation >0.5 with speed\" |\n\n### Processing Details\n- Temporal alignment: All data aligned to 2P imaging frames (~15.5 Hz)\n- dF/F computed per trial with maximin baseline (20s sliding window)\n- Deconvolved with OASIS (suite2p implementation)\n- Activity outside trial periods set to NaN (teleport periods excluded for most sessions)\n- Auto-reward on first 10 trials of new condition\n\n### Curation Steps\n\n**Neuron curation rules**:\n1. Suite2p manual curation: iscell flag (removes multi-soma ROIs, dendrites, non-responsive, overexpressing, putative interneurons)\n2. Speed-correlated interneuron exclusion: Pearson r > 0.5 between dF/F and running speed (~0.42% excluded)\n\n**Trial curation rules**:\n1. Lick sensor error: trials with >35% of frames having cumulative lick > 2 -> lick data set to NaN for that trial\n2. All valid trials (trial_number >= 0) included\n\n### Decoders Trained in Paper\n| Decoded variable | Method | Notes |\n|---|---|---|\n| Circular reward-relative position | Circular-linear regression | cos(y - y_hat) score, ~0.1-0.6 for RR cells |\n\n---\n\n## Step 4: Check for Consistency\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\n### Discrepancies Found\n| Topic | Code says | Data shows | Paper says | Resolution |\n|-------|-----------|------------|------------|------------|\n| NWB Deconvolved | Raw suite2p deconv (no NaN in ITI) | Values 0-2484, no NaN | Custom dF/F + OASIS | Must recompute dF/F from F and Fneu |\n| Neurons/session | iscell filter | 155-2281 per session | 155-2172 | Close match; slight difference may be from interneuron exclusion |\n| m18 iscell count | 2281 in iscell but only 1994 in plane0 data | Multi-plane: iscell=4857 total, 2281 cells | Planes pooled | iscell covers both planes; need to use planeIdx to map |\n| Trials per session | Some sessions have fewer: m4-ses-04=41, m4-ses-09=50, m13-ses-13=60 | 41-100 | 80.5 +/- 7.4 | Some sessions ended early; include all |\n| Environment encoding | env=0 or env=1 in NWB | -1 before scanning | ENV1=0, ENV2=1 | Use env value per trial (ignoring -1) |\n\n### Key Consistency Check\n- Paper says 11 switch mice, data has 11 subjects -> consistent\n- Paper says ~80 trials/session, data shows mean 80.4 -> consistent\n- Paper says 155-2172 neurons/session, data shows 155-2281 (before interneuron filter) -> consistent\n\n---\n\n## Step 5: Mapping Planning\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\n### Variable Mapping\n\n| Source Variable | Target Field | Transform | Notes |\n|-----------------|--------------|-----------|-------|\n| Custom dF/F + OASIS deconv of F, Fneu | `neural` | Compute dF/F with maximin baseline, deconvolve, filter by iscell | Shape: (n_neurons, n_timepoints) per trial |\n| Timestamps within trial | `input[0]`: time_from_trial_start | (frame_idx - trial_start_idx) / imaging_rate | Continuous, time-varying |\n| NWB environment | `input[1]`: environment_type | 0=ENV1, 1=ENV2 | Binary, per-trial |\n| NWB trial number | `input[2]`: trial_number | 0-indexed trial number | Continuous, per-trial |\n| Previous trial reward | `input[3]`: previous_trial_outcome | 0=omitted, 1=rewarded | Binary, per-trial |\n| Position vs reward zone | `output[0]`: distance_to_reward_zone | Discretize signed distance to nearest rz boundary into 7 bins | Time-varying |\n| NWB position | `output[1]`: absolute_position | Discretize into 5 bins of 90cm each | Time-varying |\n| NWB speed | `output[2]`: speed | Discretize into 5 bins | Time-varying |\n| NWB lick | `output[3]`: lick | Binary (any lick > 0) | Time-varying |\n| Reward zone identity | `output[4]`: reward_zone_location | 0=A, 1=B, 2=C based on zone position | Per-trial |\n| Reward delivery | `output[5]`: reward_outcome | 0=no, 1=yes | Per-trial |\n\n### Key Decisions\n\n1. **Recompute dF/F from F and Fneu**: The NWB Deconvolved data is suite2p's raw deconvolution, not the custom dF/F + OASIS used in the paper. Must recompute to match reference processing.\n\n2. **No speed threshold filtering**: The reference applies speed > 2 cm/s filter, but our decoder outputs include speed with a < 2 cm/s bin. Removing low-speed timepoints would make speed bin 0 empty. This difference from the reference is justified by the decoder specification.\n\n3. **Include ALL cells passing iscell**: The reference decoder uses cell-type subpopulations (RR, TR), but our decoder uses all cells. We apply iscell filter only (manual curation). We do NOT apply the interneuron correlation filter because: (a) it would require computing the full dF/F timeseries first and correlating with speed, (b) it only excludes ~0.42% of cells, (c) the iscell manual curation already removed obvious interneurons.\n\n4. **Time bin = imaging frame**: Each time bin is one imaging frame (~64.5 ms at 15.5 Hz). This matches the reference temporal resolution.\n\n5. **Trial definition**: Each trial runs from trial_start to teleport. Teleport/ITI periods excluded (set to NaN in reference).\n\n6. **Multi-plane animals (m17, m18)**: Pool neurons from both planes (matching reference). Neural data from plane0 and plane1 concatenated.\n\n7. **Distance to reward zone**: Compute as signed distance from position to nearest point in the reward zone [start, end]. Negative = before zone, 0 = in zone, positive = past zone. Discretize per decoder spec.\n\n8. **Reward zone determination per trial**: Determine from position where reward_zone signal > 0. Zone A: center ~105 cm, Zone B: center ~225 cm, Zone C: center ~345 cm.\n\n9. **Reward outcome per trial**: Determined from Reward timestamps array - check if any reward event falls within trial boundaries.\n\n10. **Lick sensor error correction**: Following reference, if >35% of frames in a trial have cumulative lick count > 2, set lick to 0 for that trial (matching reference behavior.py).\n\n### Planned Sanity Checks\n- [ ] Total session count matches 152\n- [ ] Neuron counts per session match range 155-2172 (after curation)\n- [ ] Trial counts per session match data\n- [ ] Reward omission rate ~15%\n- [ ] Reward zone positions match A/B/C definitions\n- [ ] Position range 0-450 cm within trials\n- [ ] Speed distribution reasonable\n- [ ] dF/F computation produces reasonable traces\n\n---\n\n## Step 6: Script Development\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\n- Full conversion script written: `/app/convert_data.py`\n- Implements custom dF/F pipeline (neuropil subtraction, maximin baseline, Gaussian smooth, OASIS deconvolution)\n- Supports `--sample`, `--full`, and `--show-processing` flags\n- Handles multi-plane animals (m17, m18)\n- All decoder inputs/outputs implemented per specification\n\n---\n\n## Step 7: Sample Conversion and Validation\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\n- Sample data (2 sessions: m11/ses-03, m12/ses-03) converted successfully\n- Data format verified with `train_decoder.py --verify-only` - Good spatial information encoded in CA1. The 7-bin discretization is fairly fine-grained; accuracy is consistent with known place cell properties.\n\n2. **absolute_position (0.65)**: 3.2x chance. Strongest spatial output. Expected - hippocampal place cells encode absolute position strongly.\n\n3. **speed (0.59)**: 3.0x chance. Speed is known to modulate hippocampal firing rates. Good decoding performance.\n\n4. **lick (0.75)**: 1.5x chance. Binary output makes this easier. Licking is anticipatory near reward zones, so neural correlates are expected.\n\n5. **reward_zone_location (0.84)**: 2.5x chance. Highest accuracy. This makes sense - after ~30 trials in each zone, the population builds strong zone-specific representations. This is consistent with the paper's finding that reward-relative cells remap to new reward locations.\n\n6. **reward_outcome (0.58)**: 1.2x chance. Weakest output but still above chance. Reward outcome is only determined at end of trial but the label is applied to all timepoints, making this harder to decode from moment-to-moment neural activity. The 85% reward rate also means the class imbalance makes balanced accuracy harder.\n\n### Potential Improvements (not implemented)\n- Per-session decoding rather than pooled across sessions (different cell populations)\n- Speed threshold filtering (but would lose speed bin 0)\n- Interneuron exclusion (minimal impact, ~0.42%)\n- More sophisticated architectures (RNN, attention)\n\n### No Issues Found\n- Data format passes all validation checks\n- All outputs above chance\n- Consistent with known hippocampal physiology\n- No obvious errors in processing pipeline\n\n---\n\n## Step 13: Documentation and Cleanup\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\n- README.md created with dataset overview, quick start, data format, and decoder results\n- All output files verified present\n- CONVERSION_NOTES.md updated through all steps\n","numLines":366,"startLine":1,"totalLines":366}}}

Extract F within trial boundaries, set rest to NaN\n    2. Neuropil subtraction: F -= NEU_COEF * Fneu\n    3. Add back neuropil mean per trial\n    4. Maximin baseline per trial\n    5. dF/F = (F - baseline) / |baseline|\n    6. Smooth dF/F with 2-sample Gaussian\n    7. Deconvolve with OASIS\n\n    Args:\n        F: (n_timepoints, n_neurons) raw fluorescence\n        Fneu: (n_timepoints, n_neurons) neuropil fluorescence\n        trial_starts: array of trial start indices (1-indexed from NWB)\n        teleports: array of teleport indices (1-indexed from NWB)\n        frame_rate: imaging frame rate\n        n_planes: number of imaging planes\n\n    Returns:\n        events: (n_neurons, n_timepoints) deconvolved events\n        dff: (n_neurons, n_timepoints) dF/F\n    \"\"\"\n    # Transpose to (n_neurons, n_timepoints) for processing\n    F = F.T.astype(np.float64)\n    Fneu = Fneu.T.astype(np.float64)\n    n_neurons, n_timepoints = F.shape\n\n    # Step 1: Extract within trials only\n    f_ = np.full_like(F, np.nan)\n    f_neu_ = np.full_like(Fneu, np.nan)\n\n    start_inds = trial_starts.tolist()\n    stop_inds = teleports.tolist()\n\n    for start, stop in zip(start_inds, stop_inds):\n        # Reference uses start-1:stop-1 (1-indexed to 0-indexed)\n        s = start - 1\n        e = stop - 1\n        if s < 0:\n            s = 0\n        if e > n_timepoints:\n            e = n_timepoints\n        f_[:, s:e] = F[:, s:e]\n        f_neu_[:, s:e] = Fneu[:, s:e]\n\n    # Step 2: Neuropil subtraction\n    f_ -= NEU_COEF * f_neu_\n\n    # Step 3-4: Baseline computation and dF/F per trial\n    nanmask = ~np.isnan(f_[0, :])\n    flow = np.full_like(f_, np.nan)\n    dff = np.full_like(f_, np.nan)\n    spks = np.full_like(f_, np.nan)\n\n    for start, stop in zip(start_inds, stop_inds):\n        s = start - 1\n        e = stop - 1\n        if s < 0:\n            s = 0\n        if e > n_timepoints:\n            e = n_timepoints\n\n        # Add back neuropil mean per trial after subtraction\n        f_[:, s:e] = f_[:, s:e] + NEU_COEF * np.nanmean(f_neu_[:, s:e], axis=1, keepdims=True)\n\n        # Maximin baseline: smooth, min filter, max filter\n        flow[:, s:e] = nansmooth(f_[:, s:e], 15, axis=1)\n        flow[:, s:e] = minimum_filter1d(flow[:, s:e], BASELINE_WINDOW, axis=-1)\n        flow[:, s:e] = maximum_filter1d(flow[:, s:e], BASELINE_WINDOW, axis=-1)\n\n    # dF/F = (F - baseline) / |baseline|\n    dff[:, nanmask] = (f_[:, nanmask] - flow[:, nanmask]) / np.abs(flow[:, nanmask])\n\n    # Step 6: Smooth dF/F with 2-sample Gaussian per trial\n    for start, stop in zip(start_inds, stop_inds):\n        s = start - 1\n        e = stop - 1\n        if s < 0:\n            s = 0\n        if e > n_timepoints:\n            e = n_timepoints\n        dff[:, s:e] = nansmooth(dff[:, s:e], DFF_SMOOTH_SIGMA, axis=1)\n\n        # Step 7: Deconvolve with OASIS\n        trial_dff = dff[:, s:e].copy()\n        trial_dff[np.isnan(trial_dff)] = 0\n        spks[:, s:e] = oasis(trial_dff, 2000, TAU, frame_rate / n_planes)\n\n    return spks, dff\n\n\ndef determine_reward_zone_per_trial(position, rz_signal, trial_starts, teleports):\n    \"\"\"\n    Determine reward zone label (A, B, C) for each trial based on\n    where the reward_zone signal is nonzero.\n","numLines":100,"startLine":56,"totalLines":812}}}

How is the `neural` data processed?\n\ni. dF/F is computed from F and Fneu following the paper's pipeline: (1) extract F within trial boundaries (set rest to NaN), (2) subtract 0.7 * Fneu, (3) add back neuropil mean per trial, (4) maximin baseline (Gaussian smooth sigma=15, min filter 300 samples, max filter 300 samples), (5) dF/F = (F - baseline) / |baseline|, (6) smooth with 2-sample Gaussian, (7) deconvolve with OASIS (tau=0.7, frame_rate/n_planes). Cells from multiple planes are concatenated. The AI does NOT use the `keep_teleports` metadata from `teleport_metadata.py` -- it always restricts the baseline to within-trial windows.\n\nii.\n```python\ndef compute_dff_and_deconvolve(F, Fneu, trial_starts, teleports, frame_rate, n_planes=1):\n    ...\n    start_inds = trial_starts.tolist()\n    stop_inds = teleports.tolist()\n    for start, stop in zip(start_inds, stop_inds):\n        s = start - 1\n        e = stop - 1\n        f_[:, s:e] = F[:, s:e]\n        f_neu_[:, s:e] = Fneu[:, s:e]\n    f_ -= NEU_COEF * f_neu_\n    ...\n    for start, stop in zip(start_inds, stop_inds):\n        ...\n        f_[:, s:e] = f_[:, s:e] + NEU_COEF * np.nanmean(f_neu_[:, s:e], axis=1, keepdims=True)\n        flow[:, s:e] = nansmooth(f_[:, s:e], 15, axis=1)\n        flow[:, s:e] = minimum_filter1d(flow[:, s:e], BASELINE_WINDOW, axis=-1)\n        flow[:, s:e] = maximum_filter1d(flow[:, s:e], BASELINE_WINDOW, axis=-1)\n    dff[:, nanmask] = (f_[:, nanmask] - flow[:, nanmask]) / np.abs(flow[:, nanmask])\n    ...\n    for start, stop in zip(start_inds, stop_inds):\n        dff[:, s:e] = nansmooth(dff[:, s:e], DFF_SMOOTH_SIGMA, axis=1)\n        ...\n        spks[:, s:e] = oasis(trial_dff, 2000, TAU, frame_rate / n_planes)\n```\n\niii. The CONVERSION_NOTES describe the dF/F pipeline accurately. The AI uses a simple nearest-center approach. The reference uses a Viterbi algorithm for more stable assignments. The AI handles missing reward zone data by using the previous trial's zone.\n\n## 7-b. What processing is involved in computing `output` *Distance to reward zone*?\n\ni. Signed distance from animal's position to the nearest edge of the assigned reward zone. Negative = before zone, 0 = in zone, positive = past zone. Then discretized into 7 bins.\n\nii.\n```python\ndef compute_distance_to_reward_zone(position, zone_start, zone_end):\n    dist = np.zeros_like(position)\n    before = position < zone_start\n    inside = (position >= zone_start) & (position <= zone_end)\n    after = position > zone_end\n    dist[before] = position[before] - zone_start\n    dist[inside] = 0.0\n    dist[after] = position[after] - zone_end\n    return dist\n```\n\niii. Standard signed distance computation. Matches the reference logic.\n\n## 7-c. How is `output` *Distance to reward zone* thresholded into categories?\n\ni. Discretized into 7 bins using explicit boolean conditions:\n- 0: < -50 cm\n- 1: -50 to -10 cm\n- 2: -10 to 0 cm\n- 3: == 0 cm (in zone)\n- 4: >0 to 10 cm\n- 5: 10 to 50 cm\n- 6: > 50 cm\n\nii.\n```python\ndef discretize_distance_to_rz(distance):\n    bins = np.zeros(len(distance), dtype=np.int64)\n    bins[distance < -50] = 0\n    bins[(distance >= -50) & (distance < -10)] = 1\n    bins[(distance >= -10) & (distance < 0)] = 2\n    bins[distance == 0] = 3\n    bins[(distance > 0) & (distance <= 10)] = 4\n    bins[(distance > 10) & (distance <= 50)] = 5\n    bins[distance > 50] = 6\n    return bins\n```\n\niii. The bins match the instructions. The reference uses `np.digitize` with a `1e-6` separator between \"0 cm\" and \">0 cm\" bins, while the AI uses explicit `== 0` and `> 0` conditions. Both correctly assign \"in zone\" to bin 3.\n\n## 7-d. How is `output` *Distance to reward zone* aligned with the neural data?\n\ni. Discretized into 5 equal-sized bins of 90 cm each spanning the 450 cm track.\n\nii.\n```python\ndef discretize_position(position):\n    bins = np.zeros(len(position), dtype=np.int64)\n    bins[position < 90] = 0\n    bins[(position >= 90) & (position < 180)] = 1\n    bins[(position >= 180) & (position < 270)] = 2\n    bins[(position >= 270) & (position < 360)] = 3\n    bins[position >= 360] = 4\n    return bins\n```\n\niii. Two-step processing: (1) Lick sensor error correction -- if more than 35% of frames in a trial have cumulative lick > 2, the entire trial's lick data is zeroed out. (2) Binarization -- any positive lick value mapped to 1.\n\nii.\n```python\nlick_corrected = lick.copy()\nfor i in range(n_trials):\n    s = trial_start_inds[i] - 1\n    e = teleport_inds[i] - 1\n    trial_lick = lick_corrected[s:e]\n    n_frames = len(trial_lick)\n    if n_frames > 0 and np.sum(trial_lick > 2) / n_frames > LICK_ERROR_THR:\n        lick_corrected[s:e] = 0\nlick_binary = (lick_corrected > 0).astype(np.int64)\n```\n\niii. The reference solution does NOT apply this correction.\n\n## 9-c. How is `output` *Lick* aligned with the neural data?\n\ni. How is the `neural` data processed?\n\ni. dF/F is computed from F and Fneu following the paper's pipeline: (1) extract F within trial boundaries (set rest to NaN), (2) subtract 0.7 * Fneu, (3) add back neuropil mean per trial, (4) maximin baseline (Gaussian smooth sigma=15, min filter 300 samples, max filter 300 samples), (5) dF/F = (F - baseline) / |baseline|, (6) smooth with 2-sample Gaussian, (7) deconvolve with OASIS (tau=0.7, frame_rate/n_planes). Cells from multiple planes are concatenated. The AI does NOT use the `keep_teleports` metadata from `teleport_metadata.py` -- it always restricts the baseline to within-trial windows.\n\nii.\n```python\ndef compute_dff_and_deconvolve(F, Fneu, trial_starts, teleports, frame_rate, n_planes=1):\n    ...\n    start_inds = trial_starts.tolist()\n    stop_inds = teleports.tolist()\n    for start, stop in zip(start_inds, stop_inds):\n        s = start - 1\n        e = stop - 1\n        f_[:, s:e] = F[:, s:e]\n        f_neu_[:, s:e] = Fneu[:, s:e]\n    f_ -= NEU_COEF * f_neu_\n    ...\n    for start, stop in zip(start_inds, stop_inds):\n        ...\n        f_[:, s:e] = f_[:, s:e] + NEU_COEF * np.nanmean(f_neu_[:, s:e], axis=1, keepdims=True)\n        flow[:, s:e] = nansmooth(f_[:, s:e], 15, axis=1)\n        flow[:, s:e] = minimum_filter1d(flow[:, s:e], BASELINE_WINDOW, axis=-1)\n        flow[:, s:e] = maximum_filter1d(flow[:, s:e], BASELINE_WINDOW, axis=-1)\n    dff[:, nanmask] = (f_[:, nanmask] - flow[:, nanmask]) / np.abs(flow[:, nanmask])\n    ...\n    for start, stop in zip(start_inds, stop_inds):\n        dff[:, s:e] = nansmooth(dff[:, s:e], DFF_SMOOTH_SIGMA, axis=1)\n        ...\n        spks[:, s:e] = oasis(trial_dff, 2000, TAU, frame_rate / n_planes)\n```\n\niii. The CONVERSION_NOTES describe the dF/F pipeline accurately. Signed distance from animal's position to the nearest edge of the assigned reward zone. Negative = before zone, 0 = in zone, positive = past zone. Then discretized into 7 bins.\n\nii.\n```python\ndef compute_distance_to_reward_zone(position, zone_start, zone_end):\n    dist = np.zeros_like(position)\n    before = position < zone_start\n    inside = (position >= zone_start) & (position <= zone_end)\n    after = position > zone_end\n    dist[before] = position[before] - zone_start\n    dist[inside] = 0.0\n    dist[after] = position[after] - zone_end\n    return dist\n```\n\niii. Standard signed distance computation. Matches the reference logic.\n\n## 7-c. How is `output` *Distance to reward zone* thresholded into categories?\n\ni. Discretized into 7 bins using explicit boolean conditions:\n- 0: < -50 cm\n- 1: -50 to -10 cm\n- 2: -10 to 0 cm\n- 3: == 0 cm (in zone)\n- 4: >0 to 10 cm\n- 5: 10 to 50 cm\n- 6: > 50 cm\n\nii.\n```python\ndef discretize_distance_to_rz(distance):\n    bins = np.zeros(len(distance), dtype=np.int64)\n    bins[distance < -50] = 0\n    bins[(distance >= -50) & (distance < -10)] = 1\n    bins[(distance >= -10) & (distance < 0)] = 2\n    bins[distance == 0] = 3\n    bins[(distance > 0) & (distance <= 10)] = 4\n    bins[(distance > 10) & (distance <= 50)] = 5\n    bins[distance > 50] = 6\n    return bins\n```\n\niii. Uses nearest-center classification. The reference uses a Viterbi algorithm for more stable across-trial assignments.\n\n## 10-b. Per trial: find frames where `reward_zone > 0`, compute mean position, classify to nearest zone center (A=105, B=225, C=345). If no reward zone signal, use the previous trial's zone (or default to A for the first trial). Encode as 0=A, 1=B, 2=C.\n\nii.\n```python\nif np.any(rz_active):\n    rz_positions = trial_pos[rz_active]\n    rz_center = np.mean(rz_positions)\nelse:\n    if len(zone_labels) > 0:\n        zone_labels.append(zone_labels[-1])\n        ...\n        continue\n    else:\n        zone_labels.append('A')\n        ...\n        continue\ndists = {k: abs(rz_center - c) for k, c in ZONE_CENTERS.items()}\nzone = min(dists, key=dists.get)\n```\n\niii. Simple nearest-center approach. Less robust than the reference's Viterbi but functionally similar for clean data.\n\n## 11-a. What variables in the raw data is `output` *Reward outcome* derived from?\n\ni. Derived from the `Reward` timestamps and `autoreward` behavior time series.\n\nii.\n```python\nreward_timestamps = bts['Reward/timestamps'][:]\n...\nrewarded = determine_reward_per_trial(reward_timestamps, behav_timestamps, trial_start_inds, teleport_inds)\nfor i in range(n_trials):\n    s = trial_start_inds[i] - 1\n    e = teleport_inds[i] - 1\n    if np.any(autoreward[s:e] > 0):\n        rewarded[i] = 1\n```\n\niii. Uses both Reward timestamps and autoreward signal.\n\n## 11-b. What processing is involved in computing `output` *Reward outcome*?\n\ni. For each trial, check if any Reward timestamp falls within the trial boundaries (by comparing timestamps). Additionally, if any autoreward > 0 within the trial, mark as rewarded. Binary: 0=no, 1=yes.\n\nii.\n```python\ndef determine_reward_per_trial(reward_timestamps, behav_timestamps, trial_starts, teleports):\n    for i in range(n_trials):\n        s = trial_starts[i] - 1\n        e = teleports[i] - 1\n        t_start = behav_timestamps[s]\n        t_end = behav_timestamps[min(e, len(behav_timestamps) - 1)]\n        in_trial = (reward_timestamps >= t_start) & (reward_timestamps <= t_end)\n        if np.any(in_trial):\n            rewarded[i] = 1\n    return rewarded\n```\n\niii. The reference does not include autoreward in the reward outcome determination.\n\n## 12. How are minor mistakes in the data, e.g. missing data, handled?\n\ni. Several cases:\n- **Empty trials**: Trials where `e <= s` are skipped.\n- **Missing reward zone data**: Trials where `reward_zone` is never active use the previous trial's zone (or default to A).\n- **NaN in neural data**: After deconvolution, NaN values are replaced with 0.\n- **Lick sensor errors**: Trials with >35% frames having lick > 2 have lick data zeroed.\n- **Trial count mismatch**: If trial_start and teleport counts differ, truncate to the shorter.\n- **Sessions with < 2 trials**: Skipped entirely.\n\nii.\n```python\ntrial_neural[np.isnan(trial_neural)] = 0\n...\nif e <= s:\n    continue\n...\nif len(neural_trials) < 2:\n    continue\n```\n\niii.
